score} function chooseAiMove(choices,roll){if(difficulty==='easy')return choices[Math.floor(Math.random()*choices.length)];let function drawVacatedSeat(color,index){ const sprite=emptySeatSprites[color];if(!sprite||!sprite.complete||!sprite.naturalWidth)return; const [sx,sy]=DOCK_IMG[color][index];const k=W/SRC_DIM; const center=rotatePoint(imageToGrid(sx,sy)); ctx.drawImage(sprite,center.x*C-65*k,center.y*C-77*k,130*k,140*k); } function drawDockPulse(point,color){const t=performance.now(),pulse=(1+Math.sin(t/180))/2; ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor=COLORS[color].hex;ctx.shadowBlur=9+12*pulse; ctx.lineWidth=2.8;ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(255,232,147,${.54+.34*pulse})`; ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(point.x*C,point.y*C-3,22+pulse*3,19+pulse*3,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()} function drawTokens(){ // Base artwork still contains its hand-rendered pawns; do not repaint them. // When a pawn leaves, a polished empty docking socket covers ONLY that pawn in the photo. players.forEach((p,pi)=>p.pieces.forEach((pos,i)=>{const isDeparting=animation&&animation.owner===pi&&animation.piece===i;if(pos!==-1||isDeparting)drawVacatedSeat(p.color,i)})); renderTokens=[]; for(const v of currentTokenPositions()){ const p=players[v.pi]; if(animation&&animation.owner===v.pi&&animation.piece===v.i)continue; const isLegal=turn===v.pi&&phase==='choose'&&!p.bot&&legal.includes(v.i); if(p.pieces[v.i]===-1){if(isLegal)drawDockPulse(v.point,p.color)} else tokenAt(v.point.x,v.point.y,COLORS[p.color],1,isLegal); renderTokens.push({...v,highlight:isLegal}); } if(animation)tokenAt(animation.x,animation.y,COLORS[players[animation.owner].color],1.09,true); } function render(){if(scene==='game'){drawBoard();drawTokens()}requestAnimationFrame(render)}requestAnimationFrame(render); canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(phase!=='choose'||current().bot||paused)return;const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();const x=(e.clientX-rect.left)/rect.width*15,y=(e.clientY-rect.top)/rect.height*15;let chosen=renderTokens.filter(t=>t.highlight).map(t=>({...t,dist:Math.hypot(t.point.x-x,t.point.y-y)})).sort((a,b)=>a.dist-b.dist)[0];if(chosen&&chosen.dist<=.62)moveToken(chosen.i)}); // Pause freezes AI decision, dice animation, token travel and optional human timer without forcing a move. let pauseStartedAt=0;function pauseGame(){if(scene!=='game'||gameOver||paused)return;pauseStartedAt=performance.now();paused=true;clearTimers();if(rules.timer&&turnDeadline){timerRemaining=Math.max(0,turnDeadline-performance.now())}transitionTicket++;if(phase==='rolling'){phase='ready';drawDie(dice)}$('pauseOverlay').classList.remove('hidden');updateUI()} let timerRemaining=0; function resumeGame(){if(!paused)return;paused=false;$('pauseOverlay').classList.add('hidden');transitionTicket++;if(rules.timer&&!current().bot)turnDeadline=performance.now()+(timerRemaining||15000);updateUI();if(phase==='moving'&&animation&&animation.resume){animation.resume()}else if(phase==='waiting'){if(pendingWait==='bonus')grantBonusAfter(450);else advanceTurnLater(450)}else if(current().bot){if(phase==='choose')scheduleAI('choose');else if(phase==='ready')scheduleAI('roll')}} $('pauseGame').onclick=pauseGame;$('resumeGame').onclick=resumeGame; function lobby(){clearTimers();transitionTicket++;moveTicket++;scene='lobby';gameOver=false;paused=false;phase='idle';animation=null;$('lobby').classList.remove('hidden');$('gameScene').classList.add('hidden');$('pauseOverlay').classList.add('hidden');$('winOverlay').classList.add('hidden');$('eventWrap').replaceChildren();refreshLobby()} $('exitGame').onclick=lobby;$('winnerLobby').onclick=lobby; $('restartGame').onclick=()=>{audioReady();beginGame()};$('playAgain').onclick=()=>{audioReady();beginGame()};$('menuBtn').onclick=()=>{if(scene==='game'){if(!paused)pauseGame();else resumeGame()}else{window.scrollTo({top:document.querySelector('.card')?.offsetTop||0,behavior:'smooth'})}}; window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&scene==='game'){e.preventDefault();paused?resumeGame():pauseGame()}if(e.key===' '&&scene==='game'&&phase==='ready'&&!current().bot){e.preventDefault();rollDice()}}); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden&&scene==='game'&&!paused&&!gameOver)pauseGame()}); setInterval(()=>{if(scene!=='game'||!rules.timer||current().bot||paused||gameOver||!['ready','choose'].includes(phase))return;const rem=turnDeadline-performance.now();$('timerFill').style.width=clamp(rem/15000*100,0,100)+'%';if(rem<=0){turnDeadline=0;if(phase==='ready')rollDice();else if(phase==='choose')moveToken(legal[0])}},130); refreshLobby();selectBoardTheme(boardTheme);drawDie(1); // Lobby mini-board uses actual playable table artwork; no unlicensed imagery. (function initLobbyPreview(){try{drawBoard();const preview=document.getElementById('lobbyBoard');if(preview){const pc=preview.getContext('2d');drawBoardImageRotated(boardArt,pc)}}catch(e){console.warn('Ludo preview fallback',e)}})(); // Diagnostics are exposed only with ?debug=1 and never determine production dice results. if(DEBUG)window.__ludoTest={get:()=>({players:structuredClone(players),turn,phase,dice,legal:legal.slice(),streak,pendingWait,rollNumber,gameOver,scene,rules:{...rules}}),set:(f)=>{f({players,turn,phase,dice,legal,streak});updateUI()},roll:rollDice,move:moveToken,board:TRACK.map(a=>a.slice()),legalMoves,capturesAt,threatened,advance:()=>{turn=(turn+1)%players.length;startTurn()},setPiece:(pi,i,pos)=>{players[pi].pieces[i]=pos;updateUI()},currentRules:()=>({...rules}),theme:()=>boardTheme,themeSelect:selectBoardTheme,safeCells:()=>TRACK.filter((_,i)=>SAFE.has(i)),themeKeys:()=>Object.keys(ROYAL_THEMES),artReady:()=>Boolean(boardTextures[boardTheme]&&boardTextures[boardTheme].naturalWidth),themeImagePrefix:()=>ROYAL_THEMES[boardTheme].image.substring(0,38),getTheme:()=>boardTheme,docks:()=>structuredClone(DOCK),spritesLoaded:()=>Object.values(pawnSprites).every(p=>p.complete&&p.naturalWidth)}; })();
